                <h1 class="gradient-text" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;" data-i18n="about.title">Our Mission</h1>
                <p class="subtitle" style="max-width: 700px; margin: 0 auto 3rem;" data-i18n="about.subtitle">
                    To provide free, uncensored, and secure internet access to everyone, everywhere. We believe information is a human right.

            <!-- Core Pillars -->
            <div class="main-grid" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap: 2rem; margin-bottom: 4rem;">

                    </div>
                    <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem;" data-i18n="about.pillar1.title">Zero Budget, Maximum Impact</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.6;" data-i18n="about.pillar1.text">
                        We operate entirely on free infrastructure (GitHub Actions/Pages). This guarantees sustainability—we can't run out of funds because we don't spend any.
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div class="card" style="padding: 2rem; border-top: 4px solid var(--brand-secondary);">
                    <div style="width: 50px; height: 50px; background: rgba(var(--brand-secondary-rgb), 0.1); border-radius: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; color: var(--brand-secondary);">
                        <i data-feather="cpu" style="width: 24px; height: 24px;"></i>
                    </div>
                    <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem;" data-i18n="about.pillar2.title">Automated Intelligence</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.6;" data-i18n="about.pillar2.text">
                        A hybrid Python/Go engine aggregates, deduplicates, tests, and "washes" thousands of proxies on a recurring schedule — fully automated.
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div class="card" style="padding: 2rem; border-top: 4px solid var(--brand-tertiary);">
                    <div style="width: 50px; height: 50px; background: rgba(var(--brand-tertiary-rgb), 0.1); border-radius: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; color: var(--brand-tertiary);">
                        <i data-feather="globe" style="width: 24px; height: 24px;"></i>
                    </div>
                    <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem;" data-i18n="about.pillar3.title">Radical Transparency</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.6;" data-i18n="about.pillar3.text">
                        Open source from day one. Every line of code, every metric, and every configuration is public and auditable by the community.
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Technology Stack (Updated) -->
            <section class="card" style="padding: 3rem; margin-bottom: 4rem;">
                <h2 style="margin-bottom: 2rem; text-align: center;" data-i18n="about.tech.title">Under the Hood</h2>

                    <div>
                        <h4 style="color: var(--brand-primary); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Backend</h4>
                        <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; color: var(--text-secondary);">
                            <li>Python 3.12 (Orchestration)</li>
                            <li>Go 1.21 (High-Speed Testing)</li>

                            <li><strong>Vwarp (Anti-Censorship)</strong></li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h4 style="color: var(--brand-secondary); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Frontend</h4>
                        <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; color: var(--text-secondary);">
                            <li>Vanilla JS (ES6+)</li>
                            <li>Web Components</li>
                            <li>Globe.gl (Visualization)</li>
                            <li><strong>WebSockets (Real-time)</strong></li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                    <div>

                        <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; color: var(--text-secondary);">
                            <li>GitHub Actions (CI/CD)</li>
                            <li>GitHub Pages (Hosting)</li>
                            <li>Docker (Containerization)</li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>

            <!-- Protocols & Clients -->
            <section class="card" style="padding: 3rem; margin-bottom: 4rem;">

                <p style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">

                </p>

    <script>
        // Feather replace handled by common-ui.js or inline-icons.js if present
        // but if not, let's keep it safe
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
             if (window.feather) feather.replace();

             // Initial set label
             const label = document.getElementById('current-lang-label');
             if(label && window.i18n) label.textContent = window.i18n.currentLang.toUpperCase();
        });

        // Listen for language changes to update UI label
        window.addEventListener('languageChanged', (e) => {
             const lang = e.detail?.lang;
             const label = document.getElementById('current-lang-label');
             if(label && lang) {
                label.textContent = lang.toUpperCase();
             }
        });
    </script>

                                    <li data-i18n="info.how.dedup" data-i18n-html="true"><strong>Deduplication:</strong> Advanced filtering removes duplicates and invalid entries</li>
                                    <li data-i18n="info.how.verify" data-i18n-html="true"><strong>Verification:</strong> A hybrid Go + Python engine tests each proxy for connectivity and speed</li>
                                    <li data-i18n="info.how.security" data-i18n-html="true"><strong>Security:</strong> Automated checks filter out honeypots, malware hosts, and unsafe nodes</li>
                                    <li data-i18n="info.how.optimize" data-i18n-html="true"><strong>Ranking:</strong> Proxies are scored by latency, reliability, and protocol intelligence</li>
                                    <li data-i18n="info.how.publish" data-i18n-html="true"><strong>Publishing:</strong> Configs are generated for all major VPN clients and three DNS profiles</li>

Supports 20+ protocols including VLESS, VMess, Trojan, Shadowsocks, Hysteria2, TUIC, and WireGuard.</p>

<h1>ConfigStream Chain Lab</h1>
<p class="subtitle">Offline Multi-Layer Chain Builder &mdash; No internet required to use this page</p>

<!-- Step 1: Layers -->
<div class="card">
  <h2>Build Your Chain (Layer by Layer)</h2>
  <p>Add layers from bottom (closest to you) to top (reaches the internet). Each layer wraps the previous one.</p>

  <div class="chain-vis" id="chainVis">
    <div class="node"><div class="icon">&#128187;</div><div class="lbl">You</div></div>
    <div class="arrow">&rarr;</div>
    <div class="node" style="border-color:var(--dim);"><div class="icon">?</div><div class="lbl">Add layers</div></div>
    <div class="arrow">&rarr;</div>
    <div class="node" style="border-color:var(--ok);"><div class="icon">&#127760;</div><div class="lbl">Internet</div></div>
  </div>

  <div id="layersContainer" class="layers-container"></div>

  <div class="btn-group">
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('socks5')">+ SOCKS5</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('http')">+ HTTP Proxy</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('warp')">+ WARP</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('vless')">+ VLESS</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('vmess')">+ VMess</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('trojan')">+ Trojan</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('ss')">+ Shadowsocks</button>
    <button class="btn-s" onclick="addLayer('fragment')">+ TLS Fragment</button>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Help -->
<div class="card">
  <h2>How This Works</h2>
  <p><strong>No internet?</strong> Start with a local proxy (Psiphon, Lantern, V2RayN) as Layer 1. Then stack WARP or your destination proxy on top.</p>
  <p><strong>Example 3-layer chain:</strong> You &rarr; SOCKS5 (local Psiphon) &rarr; WARP (clean IP) &rarr; VLESS (destination) &rarr; Internet</p>
  <p><strong>To run:</strong> Save the config, then: <code>sing-box run -c config.json</code> and set proxy to <code>socks5://127.0.0.1:2080</code></p>
  <hr>
  <p><strong>Find clean IPs offline:</strong> <code>python lab-scanner.py --scan-ips</code></p>
  <p><strong>Find local proxies:</strong> <code>python lab-scanner.py --scan-proxies</code></p>
  <p><strong>Full diagnostic:</strong> <code>python lab-scanner.py</code></p>
</div>

<script>
const WARP_PK='bmXOC+F1FxEMF9dyiK2H5/1SUtzH0JuVo51h2wPfgyo=';
let layers=[];
let lastConfig=null;

function addLayer(type){
  const id=Date.now();
  let html='';
  const fields={
    socks5:'<div class="row"><div><label>Host</label><input data-f="host" value="127.0.0.1"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="1080"></div></div>',
    http:'<div class="row"><div><label>Host</label><input data-f="host" value="127.0.0.1"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="8080"></div></div>',
    warp:'<div class="row"><div><label>Clean IP</label><input data-f="ip" value="162.159.192.1"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="2408"></div></div><div><label>WARP+ Key (optional)</label><input data-f="key" placeholder="Leave blank for free"></div>',
    vless:'<div class="row"><div><label>Server</label><input data-f="host"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="443"></div></div><div><label>UUID</label><input data-f="uuid"></div><div class="row"><div><label>SNI</label><input data-f="sni"></div><div><label>Transport</label><select data-f="net"><option value="tcp">TCP</option><option value="ws">WebSocket</option><option value="grpc">gRPC</option></select></div></div>',
    vmess:'<div class="row"><div><label>Server</label><input data-f="host"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="443"></div></div><div><label>UUID</label><input data-f="uuid"></div><div class="row"><div><label>SNI</label><input data-f="sni"></div><div><label>Transport</label><select data-f="net"><option value="tcp">TCP</option><option value="ws">WebSocket</option><option value="grpc">gRPC</option></select></div></div>',
    trojan:'<div class="row"><div><label>Server</label><input data-f="host"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="443"></div></div><div><label>Password</label><input data-f="uuid"></div><div><label>SNI</label><input data-f="sni"></div>',
    ss:'<div class="row"><div><label>Server</label><input data-f="host"></div><div><label>Port</label><input data-f="port" type="number" value="443"></div></div><div class="row"><div><label>Method</label><select data-f="method"><option>aes-128-gcm</option><option>aes-256-gcm</option><option>chacha20-ietf-poly1305</option><option>2022-blake3-aes-128-gcm</option></select></div><div><label>Password</label><input data-f="uuid"></div></div>',
    fragment:'<div class="row"><div><label>Fragment Size</label><input data-f="frag_size" value="10-30"></div><div><label>Fragment Delay (ms)</label><input data-f="frag_delay" value="5-10"></div></div><p class="hint">Applied to the layer above this one. No server needed.</p>',
  };
  html=`<div class="layer-item" data-id="${id}" data-type="${type}"><span class="layer-num">Layer ${layers.length+1}: ${type.toUpperCase()}</span><button class="rm-layer" onclick="removeLayer(${id})">&times;</button>${fields[type]||''}</div>`;
  layers.push({id,type});
  document.getElementById('layersContainer').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);
  updateVis();
}

function removeLayer(id){
  layers=layers.filter(l=>l.id!==id);
  const el=document.querySelector(`[data-id="${id}"]`);
  if(el)el.remove();
  // Renumber
  document.querySelectorAll('.layer-item').forEach((el,i)=>{
    const num=el.querySelector('.layer-num');
    if(num){const t=el.dataset.type;num.textContent=`Layer ${i+1}: ${t.toUpperCase()}`;}
  });
  updateVis();
}

function getLayerData(id){
  const el=document.querySelector(`[data-id="${id}"]`);
  if(!el)return{};
  const d={type:el.dataset.type};
  el.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(inp=>{d[inp.dataset.f]=inp.value;});
  return d;
}

function updateVis(){
  const vis=document.getElementById('chainVis');
  let html='<div class="node"><div class="icon">&#128187;</div><div class="lbl">You</div></div>';
  layers.forEach((l,i)=>{
    const icons={socks5:'&#128268;',http:'&#128268;',warp:'&#9729;&#65039;',vless:'&#128274;',vmess:'&#128274;',trojan:'&#128274;',ss:'&#128274;',fragment:'&#9986;'};
    html+=`<div class="arrow">&rarr;</div><div class="node" style="border-color:${l.type==='warp'?'var(--warn)':l.type==='fragment'?'var(--info)':'var(--brand)'}"><div class="icon">${icons[l.type]||'?'}</div><div class="lbl">L${i+1} ${l.type}</div></div>`;
  });
  html+='<div class="arrow">&rarr;</div><div class="node" style="border-color:var(--ok)"><div class="icon">&#127760;</div><div class="lbl">Internet</div></div>';
  vis.innerHTML=html;
}

function generate(){
  if(layers.length===0){showR('err','Add at least one layer.');return;}
  const port=parseInt(document.getElementById('port').value)||2080;
  const fmt=document.getElementById('fmt').value;
  const fp=document.getElementById('fp').value;
  const alpn=document.getElementById('alpn').value;
  const mux=document.getElementById('mux').value;
  const muxpad=document.getElementById('muxpad').value==='true';

  const outbounds=[];
  let prevTag=null;
  let fragOpts=null;

  // Process layers from bottom (Layer 1) to top
  for(let i=0;i<layers.length;i++){
    const d=getLayerData(layers[i].id);
    const tag=`layer-${i+1}`;

    if(d.type==='fragment'){
      fragOpts=d;
      continue;
    }

    const ob={tag};
    if(d.type==='socks5'){ob.type='socks';ob.server=d.host||'127.0.0.1';ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||1080;ob.version='5';}
    else if(d.type==='http'){ob.type='http';ob.server=d.host||'127.0.0.1';ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||8080;}
    else if(d.type==='warp'){
      ob.type='wireguard';ob.server=d.ip||'162.159.192.1';ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||2408;
      ob.local_address=[`172.16.0.${i+2}/32`];ob.private_key='YNS+CEQE6JIQiVWcOUJd0K8FLFeCQBONJnXCdFnMRlQ=';
      ob.peer_public_key=WARP_PK;ob.mtu=1280;
      if(d.key)ob.reserved=[0,0,0];
    }
    else if(d.type==='vless'){
      ob.type='vless';ob.server=d.host;ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||443;ob.uuid=d.uuid||'';ob.flow='';
      ob.tls={enabled:true,server_name:d.sni||d.host};ob.packet_encoding='xudp';
      if(d.net==='ws')ob.transport={type:'ws',path:'/'};
      else if(d.net==='grpc')ob.transport={type:'grpc',service_name:'grpc'};
    }
    else if(d.type==='vmess'){
      ob.type='vmess';ob.server=d.host;ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||443;ob.uuid=d.uuid||'';ob.security='auto';
      ob.tls={enabled:true,server_name:d.sni||d.host};
      if(d.net==='ws')ob.transport={type:'ws',path:'/'};
      else if(d.net==='grpc')ob.transport={type:'grpc',service_name:'grpc'};
    }
    else if(d.type==='trojan'){
      ob.type='trojan';ob.server=d.host;ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||443;ob.password=d.uuid||'';
      ob.tls={enabled:true,server_name:d.sni||d.host};
    }
    else if(d.type==='ss'){
      ob.type='shadowsocks';ob.server=d.host;ob.server_port=parseInt(d.port)||443;
      ob.method=d.method||'aes-128-gcm';ob.password=d.uuid||'';
    }

    // Apply evasion to proxy-type layers
    if(ob.tls&&typeof ob.tls==='object'){
      if(fp)ob.tls.utls={enabled:true,fingerprint:fp};
      if(alpn)ob.tls.alpn=alpn.split(',').map(s=>s.trim());
      if(fragOpts){
        const[ms,xs]=(fragOpts.frag_size||'10-30').split('-').map(Number);
        const[md,xd]=(fragOpts.frag_delay||'5-10').split('-').map(Number);
        ob.tls.fragment={enabled:true,size:(ms||10)+'-'+(xs||30),sleep:(md||5)+'-'+(xd||10)};
        fragOpts=null;
      }
    }
    if(mux)ob.multiplex={enabled:true,protocol:mux,max_connections:4,padding:muxpad};

    if(prevTag)ob.detour=prevTag;
    prevTag=tag;
    outbounds.push(ob);
  }

  if(outbounds.length===0){showR('err','No valid layers. Fragment alone needs a proxy layer above it.');return;}

  const primary=outbounds[outbounds.length-1].tag;

  if(fmt==='singbox'){
    lastConfig={
      log:{level:'info'},
      inbounds:[{type:'mixed',tag:'mixed-in',listen:'127.0.0.1',listen_port:port}],
      outbounds:[...outbounds,{type:'direct',tag:'direct'},{type:'block',tag:'block'}],
      route:{rules:[{inbound:['mixed-in'],outbound:primary}],final:primary}
    };
    showOutput(JSON.stringify(lastConfig,null,2),'json');
  } else if(fmt==='clash'){
    let y=`# ConfigStream Chain (Clash/Mihomo)\nmixed-port: ${port}\nallow-lan: false\n\nproxies:\n`;
    outbounds.forEach(o=>{
      y+=`  - name: "${o.tag}"\n    type: ${o.type==='socks'?'socks5':o.type}\n    server: ${o.server}\n    port: ${o.server_port}\n`;
      if(o.uuid)y+=`    uuid: ${o.uuid}\n`;
      if(o.password)y+=`    password: ${o.password}\n`;
      if(o.detour)y+=`    dialer-proxy: "${o.detour}"\n`;
      y+='\n';
    });
    y+=`proxy-groups:\n  - name: Chain\n    type: select\n    proxies:\n      - "${primary}"\n      - DIRECT\n\nrules:\n  - MATCH,Chain\n`;
    lastConfig=y;
    showOutput(y,'yaml');
  } else if(fmt==='xray'){
    const xray={log:{loglevel:'warning'},inbounds:[{tag:'socks',port:port,listen:'127.0.0.1',protocol:'socks',settings:{udp:true}}],outbounds:[]};
    const last=outbounds[outbounds.length-1];
    const xob={tag:'proxy',protocol:last.type==='socks'?'socks':last.type};
    if(last.uuid)xob.settings={vnext:[{address:last.server,port:last.server_port,users:[{id:last.uuid}]}]};
    else xob.settings={servers:[{address:last.server,port:last.server_port}]};
    xray.outbounds.push(xob,{tag:'direct',protocol:'freedom'},{tag:'block',protocol:'blackhole'});
    lastConfig=xray;
    showOutput(JSON.stringify(xray,null,2),'json');
  }
  showR('ok','Config generated! Copy or download it.');
}

function showOutput(text,type){
  const el=document.getElementById('output');
  const code=document.getElementById('outputCode');
  code.textContent=text;
  el.style.display='block';
}

function showR(type,msg){
  const el=document.getElementById('result');
  el.className='result '+type;
  el.innerHTML=msg;
}

function copyOutput(){
  const code=document.getElementById('outputCode');
  if(!code||!code.textContent)return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(code.textContent).catch(()=>{
    const r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(code);
    const s=window.getSelection();s.removeAllRanges();s.addRange(r);
    document.execCommand('copy');
  });
  showR('ok','Copied to clipboard!');
}

function downloadOutput(){
  const code=document.getElementById('outputCode');
  if(!code||!code.textContent){showR('err','Generate first.');return;}
  const fmt=document.getElementById('fmt').value;
  const ext={singbox:'.json',clash:'.yaml',xray:'.json'}[fmt]||'.json';
  const blob=new Blob([code.textContent],{type:'text/plain'});
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download='configstream-chain'+ext;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
  setTimeout(()=>{document.body.removeChild(a);URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);},100);
}
</script>
</body>
</html>

                            <input type="text" class="lab-input" id="fragDelay" value="5-10" placeholder="5-10">
                            <div class="hint">Delay between fragments. Helps evade stateful inspection.</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                        Your traffic flows: You &rarr; Layer 1 &rarr; Layer 2 &rarr; ... &rarr; Destination Proxy &rarr; Internet.
                    </div>

                <div id="chainConfigPreview" class="lab-config-output" style="display:none;">

                    <button class="lab-btn lab-btn-secondary" id="step4Next" disabled>Continue to Export</button>
                </div>

                <div class="lab-tip"><strong>Tip:</strong> If the live test is unavailable, you can still test manually. Save the config, run <code>sing-box run -c chain.json</code>, then visit <a href="https://ip.gs" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:var(--brand-primary);">ip.gs</a> through the proxy to verify your exit IP changes.</div>

                <!-- Troubleshooting -->
                <div style="margin-top: 2rem;">
                    <h3 style="font-size: 1.1rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">Troubleshooting Guide</h3>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Connection timed out</strong>
                        <p>The clean IP may be blocked by your ISP. Go back to Step 2 and try different IPs. Best ports to try: 854, 859, 864, 878, 880, 890, 891, 894, 903, 908, 928, 2408, 4500. Use <code>lab-scanner.py --scan-ips</code> to find working IP:port combos automatically.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>TLS handshake failed</strong>
                        <p>Your ISP is likely doing deep packet inspection (DPI). Solutions: 1) Enable TLS Fragment in Step 3. 2) Try the "Randomized" uTLS fingerprint. 3) Switch to ALPN "h2" only. 4) Try Double WARP strategy.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>WARP connects but proxy fails</strong>
                        <p>The inner proxy might be down or misconfigured. Try: 1) A different base proxy from Step 1. 2) Check if the proxy requires WebSocket or gRPC transport. 3) Verify the UUID/password is correct. 4) Test the proxy directly (without WARP) first.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Very slow speed</strong>
                        <p>Try a clean IP with lower latency. Use a WARP+ key for better routing. Enable multiplex (h2mux or yamux) in Advanced Evasion. Consider switching to a geographically closer proxy exit node.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Works for a while then stops</strong>
                        <p>Your ISP may be detecting and blocking the connection after a period. Try: 1) Switch to a different clean IP. 2) Enable multiplex with padding. 3) Use the Double WARP strategy. 4) Rotate clean IPs periodically.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>DNS resolution fails</strong>
                        <p>Your ISP may be hijacking DNS. In your VPN client settings, force DNS to use DoH (DNS-over-HTTPS): <code>https://1.1.1.1/dns-query</code> or <code>https://dns.google/dns-query</code>. Also try setting the remote DNS in sing-box config.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>SNI blocked for specific sites</strong>
                        <p>Some ISPs block by SNI (Server Name Indication). Use TLS Fragment strategy or ECH (Encrypted Client Hello) if supported. Reality protocol is also effective against SNI-based filtering.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- ===== STEP 5: Export ===== -->
        <div class="lab-panel lab-step-panel" id="step-5">

                <div style="margin-top: 2rem;">
                    <h3 style="font-size: 1.1rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">Quick Import Guide</h3>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Sing-Box / Hiddify</strong>
                        <p>Copy the JSON config. In Hiddify, go to Profiles &rarr; New Profile &rarr; Manual &rarr; paste the JSON content.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Clash / Mihomo</strong>
                        <p>Copy the YAML config. In Clash Verge, go to Profiles &rarr; Import from clipboard or save as .yaml file.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>V2RayN / V2RayNG / Nekobox</strong>
                        <p>Copy the raw URI. In V2RayN, click Server &rarr; Import from clipboard. For chains, use the Sing-Box JSON export.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

        <!-- ===== Offline Tools Section ===== -->
        <div class="lab-panel" style="margin-top:1rem;">
            <div class="lab-card">
                <h2>Offline Tools &amp; Scripts</h2>
                <p class="step-desc">Download these tools to run locally when you have no web access. They work offline with zero dependencies.</p>

                <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:1rem;margin-top:1rem;">
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Lab Scanner (Python)</strong>
                        <p>Full network diagnostic: find clean IPs, discover local proxies, test DNS, build multi-layer chains interactively. Works on Windows/Linux/Mac with Python 3.7+.</p>
                        <a href="tools/lab-scanner.py" class="lab-btn lab-btn-secondary" style="margin-top:0.5rem;font-size:0.8rem;" download>Download lab-scanner.py</a>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lab-trouble-item">
                        <strong>Lab Runner (Bash)</strong>
                        <p>Run and test chain configs, scan clean IPs, test individual layers. Auto-downloads sing-box. For Linux/Mac.</p>
                        <a href="tools/lab-runner.sh" class="lab-btn lab-btn-secondary" style="margin-top:0.5rem;font-size:0.8rem;" download>Download lab-runner.sh</a>
                    </div>

                        <p>A self-contained HTML file with the full chain builder. No server needed - just open in any browser.</p>

                <details style="margin-top:1.5rem;">
                    <summary style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-primary);font-size:0.95rem;">Quick Start Commands (Current)</summary>
                    <div style="padding:1rem 0 0;">
                        <div class="lab-config-output" style="max-height:340px;">
<code># Full network diagnostic (all phases)
python lab-scanner.py

# Quick connectivity check (ICMP, TCP, DNS, TLS, HTTPS)
python lab-scanner.py --quick

# Scan for clean Cloudflare IPs
python lab-scanner.py --scan-ips --top-n 20

# Include your own IPs in the scan
python lab-scanner.py --scan-ips --custom-ips "1.2.3.4:2408,5.6.7.8"

# Find working DNS servers (UDP, DoH, DoT)
python lab-scanner.py --scan-dns

# Detect SNI-based domain blocking (25 domains)
python lab-scanner.py --scan-sni

# TCP port reachability matrix (includes alt CDN IPs)
python lab-scanner.py --scan-ports

# Find local SOCKS/HTTP proxies
python lab-scanner.py --scan-proxies

# Find intranet/LAN relays with internet access
python lab-scanner.py --scan-lan

# Auto-detect best chain path (6 strategies, not just WARP)
python lab-scanner.py --auto-chain

# Auto-chain with your own proxy as Layer 1
python lab-scanner.py --auto-chain --custom-proxy socks5://127.0.0.1:1080

# Interactive multi-layer chain builder
# (paste URIs, import IPs, remove layers, mid-build testing)
python lab-scanner.py --interactive

# Test through existing proxy (HTTP + HTTPS + speed test)
python lab-scanner.py --test-proxy socks5://127.0.0.1:1080

# Export results as JSON
python lab-scanner.py --scan-ips --json > clean-ips.json</code>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </details>
            </div>
        </div>
    </main>

import logger from './utils/logger.js';

// ConfigStream Analytics - Globe & Charts
// Uses globe.gl and Chart.js

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
    // Initialize Stats - with fallback for when API isn't available
    let stats = null;
    let evasionTrend = null;

    // Try multiple data sources - metadata.json is single source of truth
    const root = window.ROOT_PATH || '';
    try {
        if (window.api && window.api.fetchStatistics) {
            stats = await window.api.fetchStatistics();
        } else {
            // Fallback: Try direct fetch from metadata.json (unified stats file)
            const response = await fetch(root + 'metadata.json?cb=' + Date.now());
            if (response.ok) {
                stats = await response.json();
            }
        }

        // Fetch evasion trend data
        try {
            const evasionResponse = await fetch(root + 'data/evasion_trend.json?cb=' + Date.now());
            if (evasionResponse.ok) {
                evasionTrend = await evasionResponse.json();
            }
        } catch (e) {
            logger.warn("Failed to load evasion trend data:", e);
        }

        if (stats) {
            updateStats(stats);
            initCharts(stats, evasionTrend);
            initGlobe(stats);
        } else {
            logger.warn("No analytics data available");
            // Show empty state when no data is available from the API
            showEmptyState();
        }
    } catch (e) {
        logger.error("Failed to load analytics data:", e);
        showEmptyState();
    }
});

function showEmptyState() {
    const container = document.getElementById('globe-viz');
    if (container) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 1.1rem;">Loading analytics data...</div>';
    }
}

// Centralized color logic
function generateColor(label, index) {
    // Consistent hashing color generation
    let hash = 0;
    const str = label.toString();
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
        hash = str.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
    }
    const hue = Math.abs(hash % 360);
    return `hsl(${hue}, 70%, 60%)`;
}

function updateStats(data) {
    const update = (id, val) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) {
            el.textContent = val;
            el.classList.remove('loading');
        }
    };

    // Helper function to format numbers with locale support
    const formatNum = (num) => {
        if (num === undefined || num === null) return 'N/A';
        if (window.i18n && window.i18n.formatNumber) {
            return window.i18n.formatNumber(num);
        }
        return num.toLocaleString();
    };

    // Use canonical fields with comprehensive fallbacks (consistent with main.js)
    // Use canonical backend field names only
    const totalSourced = data.total_lines_sourced;
    update('totalSourced', formatNum(totalSourced));

    const totalConfigs = data.total_unique_candidates;
    update('totalConfigs', formatNum(totalConfigs));

    // total_working is retested working proxies only; shielded_count is a candidate count.
    const workingCount = data.total_working ?? data.total_valid_proxies ?? data.working ?? 0;
    update('workingConfigs', formatNum(workingCount));

    const revivedWarp = data.revived_warp || 0;
    const revivedVwarp = data.revived_vwarp || 0;
    const totalRevived = data.total_revived ?? (revivedWarp + revivedVwarp);

    let cleanVal = data.total_clean;
    if (cleanVal === undefined && totalRevived !== undefined) {
        // total_clean = native working minus revived (use working/total_valid_proxies, not total_working)
        const nativeWorking = data.total_valid_proxies ?? data.working ?? 0;
        cleanVal = Math.max(0, nativeWorking - totalRevived);
    }

    update('totalClean', formatNum(cleanVal));
    update('totalRevived', formatNum(totalRevived));
    update('revivedWarp', formatNum(revivedWarp));
    update('revivedVwarp', formatNum(revivedVwarp));
    update('threatsBlocked', formatNum(data.total_dirty));

    // Smart Chains
    const smartChains = data.total_smart_chains;
    update('smartChains', formatNum(smartChains));

    // Vwarp Win Rate
    const vwarpWinRate = data.vwarp_win_rate;
    const washingEnabled = data.washing_enabled;
    const winRateDisplay = (washingEnabled !== false && vwarpWinRate !== undefined)
        ? `${Math.round(vwarpWinRate)}%` : 'N/A';
    update('vwarpWinRate', winRateDisplay);

    // Update Frequency
    const updateFreq = data.update_interval_hours || 6;
    update('updateFrequency', `${updateFreq} hrs`);

    // Shielded & Evasion stats
    update('shieldedCount', formatNum(data.shielded_count || 0));
    update('evasionUtls', formatNum(data.evasion_utls_enabled || 0));
    update('evasionDnsHardened', formatNum(data.evasion_dns_hardened_count || 0));
    update('evasionFragmentation', formatNum(data.evasion_fragmentation_enabled || 0));
    update('evasionMultiplex', formatNum(data.evasion_multiplexing_enabled || 0));

    // Update timestamp if available
    if (data.last_updated_utc) {
        const date = new Date(data.last_updated_utc);
        update('lastUpdated', date.toLocaleString());
    }
}

function initGlobe(data) {
    const container = document.getElementById('globe-viz');
    if (!container) return;

    // Show loading indicator
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 1.1rem;"><div class="spinner" style="border: 3px solid var(--border); border-top-color: var(--primary-color); border-radius: 50%; width: 40px; height: 40px; animation: spin 1s linear infinite; margin-right: 10px;"></div>Loading globe...</div>';

    // Wait for Globe.gl and THREE.js libraries to be available (retry mechanism)
    // Removed polling loop. Rely on standard load event.
    if (window.Globe && window.THREE) {
        _initGlobeInternal(data, container);
    } else {
        window.addEventListener('load', () => {
             if (window.Globe && window.THREE) _initGlobeInternal(data, container);
             else {
                 const missingLib = !window.THREE ? 'THREE.js' : 'Globe.gl';
                 logger.error(`${missingLib} library failed to load`);
                 container.innerHTML = '<div class="error-state">Visualization Unavailable (Network Error)</div>';
             }
        });
    }
}

function _initGlobeInternal(data, container) {
    // Clear loading indicator
    container.innerHTML = '';

    // Check if Globe and THREE libraries are loaded
    if (!window.Globe || typeof window.Globe !== 'function') {
        logger.error('Globe.gl library not loaded');
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; color: var(--danger-color);">Globe visualization unavailable</div>';

    if (!window.THREE) {
        logger.error('THREE.js library not loaded');
